\begin{DoxyCompactList}\small\item\em The pipe configuration field specifies both the number of pipes and how pipes are interleaved on the surface. The expression of number of pipes, the shader engine tile size, and packer tile size is encoded in a PIPE\+\_\+\+CONFIG register field. In general the number of pipes usually matches the number of memory channels of the hardware configuration. For hw configurations w/ non-\/pow2 memory number of memory channels, it usually matches the number of ROP units(? TODO\+: which registers??) The enum value = hw enum + 1 which is to reserve 0 for requesting default. \end{DoxyCompactList}\item

Addrlib device address 64-\/bit printf tag (compiler dependent) 

This allows printf to display an ADDR\+\_\+64 for either the Windows VCC compiler (which used this value) or the Gnu/\+Watcom compilers (which use \char`\"{}llx\char`\"{}. An example of use is printf(\char`\"{}addr 0x\%\char`\"{}ADDR64X\char`\"{}\textbackslash{}n\char`\"{}, address); \mbox{\Hypertarget{addrtypes_8h_a7e5b4b484f48088afb71df13773755a5}\label{addrtypes_8h_a7e5b4b484f48088afb71df13773755a5}}

\index{addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}!AddrPipeCfg@{AddrPipeCfg}}
\index{AddrPipeCfg@{AddrPipeCfg}!addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{AddrPipeCfg}{AddrPipeCfg}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily typedef enum \mbox{\hyperlink{addrtypes_8h_a68be3172f51a6a88109d356cbd0c4570}{\+\_\+\+Addr\+Pipe\+Cfg}} \mbox{\hyperlink{addrtypes_8h_a41b6c4d86868a354f041abb20c2ab4a4}{Addr\+Pipe\+Cfg}}}



The pipe configuration field specifies both the number of pipes and how pipes are interleaved on the surface. The expression of number of pipes, the shader engine tile size, and packer tile size is encoded in a PIPE\+\_\+\+CONFIG register field. In general the number of pipes usually matches the number of memory channels of the hardware configuration. For hw configurations w/ non-\/pow2 memory number of memory channels, it usually matches the number of ROP units(? TODO\+: which registers??) The enum value = hw enum + 1 which is to reserve 0 for requesting default. 

Addr\+Pipe\+Cfg \mbox{\Hypertarget{addrtypes_8h_a3a71ad42801586bbe32648f34f3d5ee7}\label{addrtypes_8h_a3a71ad42801586bbe32648f34f3d5ee7}}

\index{addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}!AddrSwizzleMode@{AddrSwizzleMode}}
\index{AddrSwizzleMode@{AddrSwizzleMode}!addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{AddrSwizzleMode}{AddrSwizzleMode}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily typedef enum \mbox{\hyperlink{addrtypes_8h_af2beb43b92a6a43e77370de1af05772d}{\+\_\+\+Addr\+Swizzle\+Mode}} \mbox{\hyperlink{addrtypes_8h_af64a81eb4c83301dba12714f6f0aefed}{Addr\+Swizzle\+Mode}}}



Neutral enums that define swizzle modes for Gfx9+ ASIC. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\end{DoxyNote}
ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+\+LINEAR linear aligned addressing mode, for 1D/2\+D/3D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+256\+B\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is 256B, for 2D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+4\+KB\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is 4KB, for 2D/3D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+64\+KB\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is 64KB, for 1D/2\+D/3D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+\+VAR\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is ASIC specific

ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+Z For GFX9\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item for 2D resource, represents Z-\/order swizzle mode for depth/stencil/\+FMask
\item for 3D resource, represents a swizzle mode similar to legacy thick tile mode For GFX10\+:
\item represents Z-\/order swizzle mode for depth/stencil/\+FMask ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+S For GFX9+\+:
\item represents standard swizzle mode defined by MS ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+D For GFX9\+:
\item for 2D resource, represents a swizzle mode for displayable resource
\item for 3D resource, represents a swizzle mode which places each slice in order \& pixel For GFX10\+:
\item for 2D resource, represents a swizzle mode for displayable resource
\item for 3D resource, represents a swizzle mode similar to legacy thick tile mode within slice is placed as 2D ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+S. Don\textquotesingle{}t use this combination if possible! ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+R For GFX9\+:
\item 2D resource only, represents a swizzle mode for rotated displayable resource For GFX10\+:
\item represents a swizzle mode for render target resource 
\end{DoxyItemize}\mbox{\Hypertarget{addrtypes_8h_ab37a829e6a079183d97179b7094ae372}\label{addrtypes_8h_ab37a829e6a079183d97179b7094ae372}}

\index{addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}!\_AddrPipeCfg@{\_AddrPipeCfg}}
\index{\_AddrPipeCfg@{\_AddrPipeCfg}!addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{\_AddrPipeCfg}{\_AddrPipeCfg}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{addrtypes_8h_a68be3172f51a6a88109d356cbd0c4570}{\+\_\+\+Addr\+Pipe\+Cfg}}}



The pipe configuration field specifies both the number of pipes and how pipes are interleaved on the surface. The expression of number of pipes, the shader engine tile size, and packer tile size is encoded in a PIPE\+\_\+\+CONFIG register field. In general the number of pipes usually matches the number of memory channels of the hardware configuration. For hw configurations w/ non-\/pow2 memory number of memory channels, it usually matches the number of ROP units(? TODO\+: which registers??) The enum value = hw enum + 1 which is to reserve 0 for requesting default.

\index{addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}!\_AddrSwizzleMode@{\_AddrSwizzleMode}}
\index{\_AddrSwizzleMode@{\_AddrSwizzleMode}!addrtypes.h@{addrtypes.h}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{\_AddrSwizzleMode}{\_AddrSwizzleMode}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily enum \mbox{\hyperlink{addrtypes_8h_af2beb43b92a6a43e77370de1af05772d}{\+\_\+\+Addr\+Swizzle\+Mode}}}



Neutral enums that define swizzle modes for Gfx9+ ASIC. 

\begin{DoxyNote}{Note}

\end{DoxyNote}
ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+\+LINEAR linear aligned addressing mode, for 1D/2\+D/3D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+256\+B\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is 256B, for 2D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+4\+KB\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is 4KB, for 2D/3D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+64\+KB\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is 64KB, for 1D/2\+D/3D resource ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+\+VAR\+\_\+$\ast$ addressing block aligned size is ASIC specific

ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+Z For GFX9\+:
\begin{DoxyItemize}
\item for 2D resource, represents Z-\/order swizzle mode for depth/stencil/\+FMask
\item for 3D resource, represents a swizzle mode similar to legacy thick tile mode For GFX10\+:
\item represents Z-\/order swizzle mode for depth/stencil/\+FMask ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+S For GFX9+\+:
\item represents standard swizzle mode defined by MS ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+D For GFX9\+:
\item for 2D resource, represents a swizzle mode for displayable resource
\item for 3D resource, represents a swizzle mode which places each slice in order \& pixel For GFX10\+:
\item for 2D resource, represents a swizzle mode for displayable resource
\item for 3D resource, represents a swizzle mode similar to legacy thick tile mode within slice is placed as 2D ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+S. Don\textquotesingle{}t use this combination if possible! ADDR\+\_\+\+SW\+\_\+$\ast$\+\_\+R For GFX9\+:
\item 2D resource only, represents a swizzle mode for rotated displayable resource For GFX10\+:
\item represents a swizzle mode for render target resource
